		icon = parliament_box

		x = 74
		y = 21

		cost = 3

		prerequisite = { focus = MDRC_LDK_state }
		prerequisite = { focus = MDRC_LDK_self_sufficient }
		prerequisite = { focus = MDRC_LDK_united_republic }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = MDRC_prereq_157_MDRC_LDK_long_term_0 has_completed_focus = MDRC_LDK_state }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = MDRC_prereq_157_MDRC_LDK_long_term_1 has_completed_focus = MDRC_LDK_self_sufficient }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = MDRC_prereq_157_MDRC_LDK_long_term_2 has_completed_focus = MDRC_LDK_united_republic }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = { tooltip = MDRC_req_156_survived has_country_flag = MDRC_LDK_survived }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = MDRC_req_156_no_succession
				NOT = { has_country_flag = MDRC_Joseph_succession }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = MDRC_req_156_leader
				has_country_leader = { name = "Laurent-Désiré Kabila" }
			}
			has_war = no
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = MDRC_req_156_no_rcd
				NOT = { country_exists = RCD }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = MDRC_req_156_no_mlc
				NOT = { country_exists = MLC }
			}
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = MDRC_req_156_reunited
				NOT = {
					any_state = {
						is_core_of = DRC
						OR = {
							NOT = { is_owned_by = DRC }
							NOT = { is_fully_controlled_by = DRC }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		completion_reward = { log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus MDRC_LDK_long_term" add_political_power = 75 }

		ai_will_do = { base = 1 }
	}
